===/root/aoci-eval/repos-r5/sympy/sympy/assumptions/===
__init__.py[LB4T]: F:Package facade re-exporting the assumptions API: Predicate, Q, ask, assuming, refine, global_assumptions and binary relation classes. | R:code:sympy/assumptions/assume.py,code:sympy/assumptions/ask.py,code:sympy/assumptions/refine.py,code:sympy/assumptions/relation/__init__.py | A:Q, ask, refine, assuming, global_assumptions, Predicate | S:Top-level sympy omits global_assumptions, BinaryRelation and AppliedBinaryRelation; import them from sympy.assumptions.
ask.py[DB7L]: F:Defines the Q predicate namespace and ask(), which evaluates a proposition under assumptions via fast paths, handlers, SAT and LRA. | R:code:sympy/assumptions/assume.py,code:sympy/assumptions/cnf.py,code:sympy/assumptions/ask_generated.py,code:sympy/assumptions/satask.py,code:sympy/assumptions/lra_satask.py | A:Q, ask, AssumptionKeys, register_handler, remove_handler | S:AssumptionKeys must hold only memoized predicate properties; fact generation iterates its __dict__. Order: single fact, handler, satask, lra_satask. Inconsistent assumptions raise ValueError.
ask_generated.py[FB3M]: F:Auto-generated tables of known facts between unary predicates as CNF clauses and implied/rejected dictionary. | R:code:sympy/assumptions/facts.py,code:sympy/assumptions/ask.py | A:get_all_known_facts, get_all_known_matrix_facts, get_all_known_number_facts, get_known_facts_dict | S:Generated; edit facts.py, rerun bin/ask_update.py.
assume.py[DB6L]: F:Defines Predicate, AppliedPredicate, UndefinedPredicate, AssumptionsContext, global_assumptions and the assuming context manager. | R:code:sympy/multipledispatch/dispatcher.py,code:sympy/logic/boolalg.py | A:Predicate, AppliedPredicate, UndefinedPredicate, AssumptionsContext, global_assumptions, assuming | S:assuming() restores global_assumptions via clear and update on exit. Predicate(...) returns UndefinedPredicate with no dispatch; its eval is deprecated. AppliedPredicate.arg only for unary.
cnf.py[KB5L]: F:Internal CNF machinery: Literal, AND/OR, NNF conversion, CNF distribution and EncodedCNF integer encoding for the SAT solver. | R:code:sympy/assumptions/assume.py,code:sympy/assumptions/facts.py,code:sympy/logic/boolalg.py | A:CNF, EncodedCNF, Literal, to_NNF, distribute_AND_over_OR | S:CNF.extend returns self but add_clauses mutates in place. EncodedCNF.encode maps S.false to 0. CNF._not uses clauses[-1] and fails on empty CNF. Literal.__eq__ assumes other has .arg.
facts.py[DB5M]: F:Defines the known logical facts between unary number and matrix predicates, composite predicate expansions and generator helpers for ask_generated. | R:code:sympy/assumptions/ask.py,code:sympy/assumptions/ask_generated.py,code:sympy/assumptions/cnf.py | A:get_known_facts, get_composite_predicates, generate_known_facts_dict, get_known_facts_keys | S:Regenerate ask_generated.py on fact changes. get_known_facts_keys lists Q attributes except eq/ne/gt/lt/ge/le. ask_full_inference returns None if undecided; single_fact_lookup is O(n^2) SAT calls.
lra_satask.py[KB4M]: F:Evaluates propositions with a SAT solver plus linear real arithmetic theory to handle inequality predicates. | R:code:sympy/assumptions/cnf.py,code:sympy/assumptions/ask.py,code:sympy/logic/algorithms/lra_theory.py | A:lra_satask, WHITE_LIST | S:Raises UnhandledInput for non-whitelisted predicates, integer, matrix or NaN exprs; ask() catches it, returns None.
refine.py[DB5L]: F:Implements refine(), which simplifies expressions under assumptions using per-class handlers for Abs, Pow, atan2, re, im, arg, sign and MatrixElement. | R:code:sympy/assumptions/ask.py,code:sympy/assumptions/__init__.py | A:refine, handlers_dict | S:Handlers are looked up by class name string in handlers_dict, so new handlers need a string key. Recurses on args first. Handlers returning None leave expr unchanged.
satask.py[KB5M]: F:Evaluates a proposition with SAT by recursively gathering class facts and known facts for relevant expressions. | R:code:sympy/assumptions/sathandlers.py,code:sympy/assumptions/cnf.py,code:sympy/assumptions/ask_generated.py | A:satask, get_all_relevant_facts, get_relevant_clsfacts, extract_predargs | S:Known facts are replicated once per expression with shifted literal indices, so cost grows with expression count. Both sides unsat raises ValueError. iterations=oo loops until no new exprs.
sathandlers.py[DB4M]: F:Registers per-class logical facts for Abs, Add, Mul, MatMul, Pow and numbers used by the SAT-based assumption solver. | R:code:sympy/assumptions/ask.py,code:sympy/assumptions/satask.py | A:class_fact_registry, ClassFactRegistry, allargs, anyarg, exactlyonearg | S:Lookup is by type(expr) plus issubclass over registered classes.
wrapper.py[DD4S]: F:Provides AssumptionsWrapper and is_infinite/is_extended_real/is_extended_nonnegative so old .is_x attributes and new ask() queries share one syntax. | R:code:sympy/assumptions/ask.py,code:sympy/core/basic.py | A:AssumptionsWrapper, is_infinite, is_extended_real, is_extended_nonnegative, make_eval_method | S:AssumptionsWrapper(expr) without assumptions returns expr itself. is_ helpers use ask only if assumptions given.
